# Full per-model compound perturbation census (all 415 models, 1,458 compounds)

Generated by a calibrated Python classifier that scanned every
`*/*_mrgsolve_model.R` file for each drug's own PK-derived concentration
variable, and classified how it could be redirected to an externally-supplied
covariate (see `../../ESQlabs_curation_and_extension.md` and the pilot plan
this table was produced for). The classifier reproduced all 6 hand-verified
ground-truth files exactly before being trusted at this scale.

**Known limitations, read before using this table for anything beyond
triage:**
- A second, independent reimplementation of this same classifier produced
  materially different aggregate totals in the long tail beyond the 6-file
  calibration set (most notably on `delete_compartment` classification and
  overall per-file detection rate), despite both matching the calibration
  set exactly. Treat individual row classifications as a strong first pass,
  not ground truth — especially near category boundaries.
- Target/Pathway is genuinely unknown (`?`) for roughly 70% of compounds —
  the lookup dictionary only covered a minority of the ~1,450 distinct drug
  names found across the corpus. `?` means "not determined," never a guess.
- A few duplicate rows exist where the same compound was independently
  detected twice within one file (e.g. `visceral-leishmaniasis | Miltefosine`,
  `chagas-disease | Nifurtimox and fexinidazole`) — worth a quick manual check
  before treating them as two separate targets.

Last three columns are intentionally blank placeholders for manual
annotation (e.g. target compartment, expected unit, status), sorted by
disease directory then compound name.

| acute-intermittent-porphyria | HEM | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | ALAS1 (heme feedback) | LIV_HEM | C_HEM (nmol/g liver) / EFFECT_HEM (fraction 0-1) | Refactored 2026-08-30: Archetype 1 (no depot, single central compartment, linear elimination; CENT_HEM dosed/read directly as a concentration, same quirk as TOCI_C) plus a bespoke second liver-tissue compartment (LIV_HEM, first-order hepatic uptake + HO-1 catabolism). C_HEM=LIV_HEM is a straight alias. EFFECT_HEM is a rename via an exact algebraic identity, not a fit: original's HEM_EFF=1/(1+(C/EC50_HEM)*KALAS1_HEM) is Hill-shaped once solved for its complement, EFFECT_HEM = X/(EC50_HEM+X) with X=KALAS1_HEM*C_HEM, EMAX_HEM=1.0/GAMMA_HEM=1.0 added explicit (both algebra-forced by the original, not invented); EC50_HEM/KALAS1_HEM keep original names+values unchanged. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api on the same Scenario 4 combo run as GIV above: all HEM-related outputs match to floating-point noise (HEMIN_PLASMA/CENT_HEM max abs diff 3.6e-11, a large reported relative diff there is a near-zero/near-zero artifact post-clearance, not a real mismatch). C_HEM/EFFECT_HEM are $ODE-local + $CAPTURE only, not $PARAM (same mrgsolve 2.0.1 constraint as GIV above). See aip_refactor_notes.md for the full algebraic derivation and the 2 upstream defects logged (UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #39, shared with GIV, unrelated to either compound). |

| alagille-syndrome | BA | Not a drug -- endogenous disease state | Bile acid, the enterohepatic-circulation substrate the whole model turns on, produced by the model's own synthesis term SYN=SYN0*CYP | _ | _ | Checked per the task's specific flag: BA spans seven $CMT compartments (BAHEP/BABIL/BADUO/BAILE/BAPOR/BASYS/BACOL) forming the model's bile-acid enterohepatic circulation; no ev()/dataset dosing route targets any BA* compartment anywhere in the file's 16 scenarios or R driver (confirmed by exact-name grep). | alagille-syndrome | Terminal ileum | Not a compound -- anatomical site name, not a drug identifier | N/A | N/A | N/A | Checked per the task's specific flag: "Terminal ileum" is not a compound name anywhere in the file -- it is anatomical-site text lifted from the $CMT @annotated comment on two different, unrelated compartments (`DILE : IBAT inhibitor, terminal ileum - site of action (ug)` and `BAILE : Terminal ileal luminal bile acid (umol)`). Neither compartment is itself named "Terminal ileum". Same class of scanner error as neonatal-hyperbilirubinemia's census mislabeling and essential-tremor's "Organ systems (PRM)" row (a process-description phrase mistaken for a drug name). No PK refactor performed for this row; see algs_refactor_notes.md |
| alagille-syndrome | IBAT inhibitor (maralixibat/odevixibat) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | ASBT / SLC10A2 (ileal bile acid transporter) | C_IBAT (ug, potency-scaled ileal drug amount -- an amount, not a concentration; no volume of distribution exists anywhere in the original for this compound) / EFFECT_IBAT (fraction 0-1) | ug (ileal amount) | Missed entirely by the census (undercounted, same failure mode as prostate-cancer and hypercalcemia-of-malignancy). Bespoke archetype: none of the guide's four reference shapes fit a linear 3-compartment GI transit chain (GUT_IBAT->MIDGUT_IBAT->ILE_IBAT, was DEPOT->DGUT->DILE) feeding a small PD-inert systemic branch (SYS_IBAT, was DSYS), all in raw mass units with no plasma concentration or volume term anywhere in the original -- kept as disclosed micro-rate-constant transit (KTR1_IBAT/KTR2_IBAT/KTR3_IBAT/KSYSD_IBAT), same rationale as this file's ZOL precedent in hypercalcemia-of-malignancy. Dosed as a continuous zero-order $ODE input (DOSEKG_IBAT/TSTART_IBAT/TSTOP_IBAT), the guide's own "continuous infusion input, not an event" edge case, differentiated by a potency multiplier POT_IBAT (odevixibat 1.00, maralixibat 0.28 -- the file's own ODEV/MRX shorthands) rather than by separate compartments per drug. EFFECT_IBAT is an exact rename of the original's plain Emax-ratio INH (EMAX_IBAT was IMAXA, GAMMA_IBAT=1 new/math-implied, no fit). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api on both of the file's own primary trial-replication scenarios (sc02_iconic, maralixibat 380ug/kg/d, 438.3d window; sc03_assert, odevixibat 120ug/kg/d, both drug and placebo arms, 180d window), all at native delta=1d, no shortening needed: exact match, max abs diff 0.0 across all 61 shared outputs, every scenario. No pre-existing upstream mrgsolve-2.0.1 build defect -- original compiles cleanly, no UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md entry needed. See algs_refactor_notes.md |

| atrial-fibrillation | Amiodarone | Normalize duplicate concentration sites, then redirect | ERP/IKr/ICaL (electrical remodeling) + HR (chronotropy) | C_AMIO / EFFECT_AMIO_ERP, EFFECT_AMIO_HR, EFFECT_AMIO_IKR, EFFECT_AMIO_CA | ug/mL (plasma) | Refactored: archetype 3 (depot+central+peripheral), GUT_AMIO/CENT_AMIO/PERI_AMIO (was GI_AMIO/C1_AMIO/C2_AMIO). K12_AMIO/K21_AMIO kept literal (not mass-balanced: k12*V1=0.8 vs k21*V2=0.84, so forcing a single Q would change the calibrated trajectory -- disclosed, see notes). No desethylamiodarone or any other metabolite modelled in this file (checked from code, not assumed). Four independent EFFECT_AMIO_* terms (ERP/HR/IKr/ICaL), each a rename of an already-plain Hill ratio, gamma=1. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api: original's own S3_rhythm_amio scenario (400mg q12h x28d load -> 200mg q24h maintenance), full 730d/17520h, exact match (max abs diff 0.0) over 2193 pts, all 30 shared $CAPTURE outputs; also exact in the combined S6_amio_apix scenario. Original file had 3 pre-existing mrgsolve 2.0.1 build defects unrelated to any compound's own PK (17 $CMT names duplicated in $CAPTURE; Cp_METRO_ng double-declared in both $ODE and $TABLE; $PARAM IL6_0 colliding with mrgsolve's auto <cmt>_0 symbol for compartment IL6) -- logged as UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #128, fixed as syntax-only in the refactored sibling. See af_refactor_notes.md |

| autoimmune-encephalitis | CPX | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Lymphocyte/plasma-cell cytotoxicity (4-OH-cyclophosphamide, alkylating active metabolite) | C_CPX / EFFECT_CPX | ng/mL (V1) | Refactored: archetype 1 (single compartment), CENT_CPX (was CPX_ACT). Genuine in-scope PK bug found and fixed: the original silently reused CPX_ACT as tocilizumab's own peripheral compartment, so CPX's own declared CL_CPX/Vc_CPX were dead code -- CPX actually decayed ~95x slower than declared, and dosing CPX alone drove the file's own reported TCZ concentration to 6.86e9 by day 365 in a scenario with zero mg of tocilizumab. Fixed using CPX's own already-declared CL_CPX/V1_CPX (no new numbers), restoring the original's own commented-out, never-wired formula. EFFECT_CPX renamed from a plain Emax*C/(EC50+C) ratio, gamma=1 (rename, not a fit). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api: original's own scenario 5 (Cyclophosphamide, day 30 q28d x6) over the full 365d/delta=0.5 grid -- large, expected divergence on disease outputs (this fix), disclosed in full. See aie_refactor_notes.md and UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #140 |
| autoimmune-encephalitis | IVIG | Normalize duplicate concentration sites, then redirect | FcRn saturation -> accelerated endogenous IgG catabolism | C_IVIG / EFFECT_IVIG | mg/mL-equivalent (V1, x1000 convention) | Refactored: archetype 2 (2-compartment, no depot), CENT_IVIG/PERI_IVIG (was IVIG1/IVIG2). EFFECT_IVIG renamed from a plain Emax*C/(EC50+C) ratio, gamma=1. | autoimmune-encephalitis | TCZ | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | IL-6 receptor -- IL-6 signaling blockade (plain concentration-driven Hill, no receptor-binding ODE; not TMDD) | C_TCZ / EFFECT_TCZ | mcg/mL (V1) | Refactored: archetype 2 (2-compartment, no depot), CENT_TCZ/PERI_TCZ (was TCZ1, PERI_TCZ newly dedicated). Genuine in-scope PK bug found and fixed: the original never gave TCZ its own peripheral state -- it silently reused the CPX_ACT compartment ("reusing CPX_ACT slot for TCZ2 peripheral"), using the wrong volume plus a spurious elimination term, causing the original's own reported TCZ concentration to blow up unboundedly (1.74e9 by day 365 in the file's own Tocilizumab scenario, 6.86e9 in the Cyclophosphamide-only scenario where TCZ is never dosed at all). Fixed with a real, dedicated PERI_TCZ using TCZ's own already-declared CL_TCZ/V1_TCZ/V2_TCZ/Q_TCZ (no new numbers) -- refactored C_TCZ now shows a physiologically sane rise-then-decay instead. EFFECT_TCZ renamed from a plain Emax*C/(EC50+C) ratio, gamma=1 (confirmed not TMDD -- no receptor pool/complex compartment in the original). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api: original's own scenario 6 (Tocilizumab, day 60 q28d x6), full 365d/delta=0.5 grid -- large, expected divergence on disease outputs and the TCZ concentration column (this fix), disclosed in full; RTX/MP/IVIG outputs in the same scenario remain exact. See aie_refactor_notes.md and UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #140 |

Archetype 2; harmonized the original's own inconsistent PEM-vs-PEMBRO stems to PEM (CENT_PEM/PERI_PEM), matching its own $PARAM block. The original's checkpoint-inhibition effect (CD8+ boost, ICI-enhanced kill, HPV clearance boost) is driven entirely by a binary ICI_flag x CPS_high switch (renamed EFFECT_PEM, same formula) -- never a function of C_PEM, which is exposed but unconsumed by any disease equation; not fabricated as a new dependency the original never had. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api across all 6 scenarios: exact match, max abs diff 0.0. Same 3-defect build-compat fix as the Bevacizumab row, same UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #76 entry |
| cervical-cancer | TV-ADC | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Tissue factor / MMAE-microtubules | C_TVADC / EFFECT_TVADC (driven by the released payload MMAE_TVADC, not C_TVADC directly) | µg/mL conjugate (plasma, IV); payload relative units | Bespoke, not any of the 4 archetypes: Archetype-2 conjugate PK base (CENT_TVADC/PERI_TVADC) plus a 3rd, non-TMDD payload compartment (MMAE_TVADC, was MMAE_free) fed by a named fraction (FR_MMAE_TVADC=0.4, was hardcoded) of the conjugate's own clearance flux, independent first-order decay. EFFECT_TVADC renamed from an inline MMAE_free/(MMAE_free+1.0) ratio already Hill-shaped (EMAX_TVADC=1/EC50_TVADC=1/GAMMA_TVADC=1) -- rename, not a fit; deliberately built on the payload, not the conjugate concentration, reflecting real ADC pharmacology. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api across all 6 scenarios: exact match, max abs diff 0.0. Same 3-defect build-compat fix as the Bevacizumab row, same UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #76 entry |

Original computed plasma concentration twice under two names (Cop in $ODE, independently-derived Opioid_Cplasma in $TABLE) - consolidated into one C_OPIOID, $GLOBAL-predeclared and assigned (not redeclared) at both sites per mrgsolve's cross-block hoisting constraint (same fix as C_MIT in hsph). Original did not compile at all ($CAPTURE re-lists 9 $INIT compartment names) - logged UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #111, syntax-only fix (removed from $CAPTURE, still auto-output as states) applied in cp_mrgsolve_model_refactored.R. Vancomycin selected as the row's referent (first-listed in every section, only one named in the model's own docstring, used in 8/18 scenarios incl. the "standard of care" one); fidaxomicin/metronidazole/rifaximin/ridinilazole/index antibiotic remain out of scope, untouched, candidates for a separate future row. Bespoke 3-stage GI-transit chain (GUT_VAN->GUT2_VAN->CENT_VAN, no systemic compartment -- oral vancomycin is not absorbed), not a clean archetype 1-3. EFFECT_VAN is an exact rename of the original's FV ratio (EMAX=1/GAMMA=1 promoted, not fit). | controlled-ovarian-stimulation | AGO (triptorelin, the GnRH agonist trigger -- named explicitly in the original's own parameter comments) | Normalize duplicate concentration sites, then redirect | GnRH receptor (agonist; competes with ANT/ganirelix for one site) | GUT_AGO/CENT_AGO | C_AGO (ng/mL) / EFFECT_AGO (fractional GnRH-receptor occupancy by the agonist; genuine competitive-binding rename, not a fit -- reads both C_AGO and C_ANT) | Refactored: archetype 3 minus peripheral (depot+central); bioavailability pre-applied to the dosed amount as in the original (no F_AGO term). Normalized the original's own duplicate $ODE/$TABLE concentration re-derivations into one $GLOBAL macro (the "duplicate concentration sites" this row was flagged for). EFFECT_AGO/EFFECT_ANT are disclosed bespoke competitive GnRH-receptor occupancy (XOCC_AGO/(1+XOCC_AGO+XOCC_ANT)), kept as two separate named interfaces sharing both drugs' concentrations rather than force-collapsed into one. Pre-existing mrgsolve-2.0.1 build defect (loop-counter hoisting collision + multi-declarator hoisting, unrelated to any compound's own PK) fixed syntax-only in the delivered _refactored.R, logged as UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #132. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api: hi-ago-freeze scenario (PCOS patient, agonist trigger, no luteal support) and the corifollitropin scenario (which also exercises ANT/HCG), exact match (max abs diff 0.0) across 28 shared/renamed outputs, 121 and 108 points -- see cos_refactor_notes.md |

| drug-induced-liver-injury | APAP (acetaminophen/paracetamol) | Missing from census — added by this refactor (structurally: redirect concentration, clean single site plus one bespoke tissue site) | CYP-mediated bioactivation to a reactive metabolite (hepatotoxicity trigger); BSEP canalicular transporter (cholestasis liability) | GUT_APAP/CENT_APAP/PERI_APAP/LIV_APAP | C_APAP (plasma, uM) / CU_APAP (unbound hepatic, uM) / EFFECT_APAP (uM/h) / EFFECT_APAP_BSEP (fraction) | Refactored 2026-08-31: the file's own culprit hepatotoxin (dosed via AGUT/AC/AP/ALIV, the model's only drug-dosing route) had no census row at all. Archetype 3 (depot+central+peripheral, linear) plus a bespoke 4th tissue compartment (LIV_APAP) since hepatic bioactivation reads a genuinely different concentration (intrahepatic unbound, CU_APAP) than the plasma PK (C_APAP). Two disease-effect hookups named (EFFECT_APAP = reactive-metabolite production flux; EFFECT_APAP_BSEP = competitive BSEP-inhibition factor), neither force-fit into an Emax/EC50 Hill — EFFECT_APAP is a rename of the original's own prod_RM term, deliberately not Hill-shaped because the file's own thesis is that bioactivation must NOT saturate like conjugation does (see dili_refactor_notes.md). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against two of the original's own scenarios (S5 "350 mg/kg + NAC at 8h", S10 "Drug B (BSEP inhibitor) 8 mg/kg q12h x28d" which activates the BSEP pathway), both exact match to floating-point-solver noise. One pre-existing upstream mrgsolve-2.0.1 build defect found and fixed syntax-only (UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #113), disclosed in dili_refactor_notes.md |

| graves-disease | MMI (methimazole) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Thyroid peroxidase (TPO) inhibition; disease-specific B-cell suppression | C_MMI / EFFECT_MMI | ug/mL-equivalent (concentration-state compartment, no volume division in the original) | Confirmed genuinely drivable (own compartment `MMI`, dosed via `ev(cmt=1,...)` in 4 of the file's 6 scenarios). Archetype 1 (no depot, single compartment, linear elimination), bespoke: the original's own compartment IS the concentration (Vd_MMI/ka_MMI/F_MMI all declared but never used in $ODE), so C_MMI is an identity of CENT_MMI, not a division. EFFECT_MMI (rename, gamma=1, EMAX=1 math-implied) feeds two downstream sites (TPO inhibition, B-cell suppression) sharing one EC50, same as the original. Original does not compile under mrgsolve 2.0.1 (F_MMI collides with mrgsolve's reserved F_<cmt> symbol; $CAPTURE re-lists 8 compartment names) — logged UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #93, fixed as a disclosed syntax-only change in the refactored sibling. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against scenarios 2/4/6 (MMI, MMI+Propranolol, Rituximab+MMI), exact match (max abs diff 0.0, deterministic outputs) — see gd_refactor_notes.md |
| graves-disease | PROP (propranolol) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | beta-adrenergic receptor (D1-deiodinase inhibition + heart-rate reduction) | C_PROP / EFFECT_PROP | ug/mL-equivalent (concentration-state compartment) | Confirmed genuinely drivable (own compartment `PROP_C`, dosed via `ev(cmt=5,...)` in scenario 4). Archetype 1, same bespoke concentration-state deviation as MMI (Vd_PROP/ka_PROP/F_PROP declared, unused). One EFFECT_PROP (rename, gamma=1, EMAX=1 math-implied) shared by both downstream uses (D1 inhibition, HR reduction), matching the original's own shared EC50_PROP_HR (renamed EC50_PROP). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against scenario 4, exact match (max abs diff 0.0) — see gd_refactor_notes.md |
| graves-disease | PTU (propylthiouracil) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Thyroid peroxidase (TPO) inhibition + D1-deiodinase inhibition (two distinct affinities) | C_PTU / EFFECT_PTU, EFFECT_PTU_D1 | ug/mL-equivalent (concentration-state compartment) | Confirmed genuinely drivable — full PK block (own compartment `PTU_C`, own kel_PTU, own IC50_PTU/IC50_PTU_D1) identical in kind to MMI/PROP, but none of the file's own 6 scenarios doses it. Archetype 1, same bespoke concentration-state deviation. Genuinely two different receptor affinities in the original (EC50_PTU=1.0 for TPO inhibition, EC50_PTU_D1=5.0 for D1 inhibition) — kept as two named Hill interfaces rather than force-collapsed into one, per the guide's "don't force a fit" principle. Verified via one disclosed, explicitly non-original dosing scenario (no shipped scenario exists to dose it), exact match (max abs diff 0.0) — see gd_refactor_notes.md |

| hashimoto-thyroiditis | Levothyroxine (LT) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Substrate replacement into endogenous plasma T4 pool (HPT axis) -- not receptor-mediated | C_LT4 (central conc.) / EFFECT_LT4 (source term into T4p; not Hill-shaped, see notes) | ug/L (C_LT4) / nmol/L/day (EFFECT_LT4) | Refactored: archetype 3 (depot+central+peripheral, linear), continuous zero-order infusion input (not an event, preserved from original). CL_LT4/V1_LT4/Q_LT4/V2_LT4 in CL/Q/V form (Q_LT4/V2_LT4 derived by exact algebraic identity from original's k12_LT4/k21_LT4, same recipe as chronic-hypothyroidism/hypo_refactor_notes.md's own LT4). EFFECT_LT4 is a substrate-replacement source term into T4p (exact rename of original's LT4c_nmol*kel_LT4, same finding as hypo's LT4 -- no EMAX/EC50/GAMMA invented). 3 pre-existing mrgsolve-2.0.1 build defects found and fixed in the refactored sibling only (F_Se reserved-symbol collision, $CAPTURE re-listing 13 compartment names, 5 $PARAM names colliding with mrgsolve's auto <cmt>_0 init symbols), logged UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #116. Key finding: $MAIN-vs-$ODE placement of the state-dependent source term is numerically load-bearing here (confirmed empirically) -- kept in $MAIN, same position as original, not moved to $ODE. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against the original's own scenarios 1/2/5/6 (5yr/weekly): exact match (max abs diff 0.0) throughout, including identical pre-existing disease-side NaN blow-up timing in 3/4 scenarios (1 scenario shows the blow-up 1 week earlier in an unrelated, non-LT4-coupled autoimmune sub-block, consistent with floating-point sensitivity of an already-chaotic uncapped feedback loop, not a structural error -- see ht_refactor_notes.md) |
| hashimoto-thyroiditis | Liothyronine (LIT) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) [corrected from "Delete PK compartment" -- LiT3c is a real mass-state compartment requiring division by Vc_LiT3 to reach a concentration, not already a concentration] | Substrate replacement into endogenous plasma T3 pool (HPT axis) -- not receptor-mediated | C_LIT3 (central conc.) / EFFECT_LIT3 (source term into T3p; not Hill-shaped, see notes) | ug/L (C_LIT3) / nmol/L/day (EFFECT_LIT3) | Refactored: archetype 1 (no depot, single compartment, linear), continuous zero-order infusion input (edge case, same as LT4). Disclosed, uncorrected unit inconsistency copied verbatim from original (comment says T3 MW=651 Da, code divides by 65.5). | hereditary-spherocytosis | Mitapivat | Normalize duplicate concentration sites, then redirect | PKR (pyruvate kinase-R) | C_MIT / EFFECT_MIT_ATP, EFFECT_MIT_DPG (shared OCC_MIT occupancy) | ng/mL (plasma) | Refactored: archetype 3 (depot+central+peripheral, linear CL/Q/V, `*24` h->day conversion kept). Duplicate-site defect was `conc` ($ODE, feeding `drv`) vs `MITA`'s inline formula ($TABLE) both independently computing CENT/Vc*1000 — normalized into one `$GLOBAL`-cached `C_MIT`, assigned identically at both sites (mrgsolve 2.0.1 forbids `$PARAM` here, same constraint as breast-cancer/AMD/membranous-nephropathy/cervical-cancer/stable-angina). `OCC_MIT`=C/(EC50+C) (GAMMA_MIT=1, rename not a fit) feeds two separate named effects (EMAX_MIT_ATP, EMAX_MIT_DPG). Original didn't compile at all (4 unrelated pre-existing defects: stray duplicate `@annotated`, undeclared N_1..N_9, 14 multi-declarator `double a=,b=;` lines losing type, and 11 cross-block scratch-name collisions from mrgsolve's variable hoisting) — logged as `translations/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md` #107, syntax-only fixes applied directly in `hsph_mrgsolve_model_refactored.R`. No R wrapper in this file (bare mread() DSL) so no `.cpp` extraction needed, same as drta/ted. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api: exact match (max abs/rel diff 0.0) on all 29 outputs, 1401 pts (t=0-1400d), both no-drug and the file's own 100 mg BID scenario (from hsph_shiny_app.R) — see hsph_refactor_notes.md |

| huntingtons-disease | DTBZ | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | VMAT2 (same target as HTBZ/VBZ) -- NOT a parent-metabolite chain with HTBZ despite real-world deutetrabenazine pharmacology; see refactor notes | C_DTBZ (== PERI_DTBZ) / EFFECT_DTBZ | compartment amount, not divided by volume -- see notes | Dosed directly into its own single central compartment (no GUT_/depot; KA_DTBZ/F_DTBZ declared in the original but never wired into a dxdt_ line, preserved unused). Archetype 1 (no depot, single compartment, linear elimination) plus a one-way, non-mass-conserving brain-exposure compartment (PERI_DTBZ) -- structurally identical in shape to VBZ/RILUZOLE's own brain compartments in this file, but independent from HTBZ (no shared state, no conversion term); flagged as a modeling simplification relative to deutetrabenazine's real pharmacology (its own deuterated dihydrotetrabenazine metabolite), not a defect, since the original never claims otherwise. EFFECT_DTBZ split out from the original's shared 3-drug VMAT2 Hill curve into its own copy (same values as HTBZ/VBZ), combined only at the point of use (Bliss independence). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api on the original's own DTBZ_30mg and Combo_DTBZ_Tominersen scenarios, exact match (max abs diff 0.0). Two pre-existing build defects found (unrelated to any compound's own PK, syntax-only fixed in the delivered file): $CMT+$INIT jointly redeclaring all 20 compartments, and an $ODE call to an R-only function undefined at compile time -- logged translations/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #87; entire $TABLE block never captured -- logged #88 |
| huntingtons-disease | HTBZ | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | VMAT2 (vesicular monoamine transporter 2; inhibits synaptic dopamine reuptake into vesicles, reducing chorea) -- TBZ's own active metabolite, not an independent compound (parent-metabolite chain, same handling as sarcoidosis's PRED/PREDL and urolithiasis's ALLO/OXP pairs; corrected from `?`) | C_HTBZ / EFFECT_HTBZ | compartment amount, not divided by V1_HTBZ -- original exposes the raw mg quantity as "concentration", scaled x1000 in the Hill term as if uM (see notes) | Corrected from `?` -- confirmed genuine parent-metabolite chain: HTBZ has no depot/absorption params of its own; its only inflow is a conversion term out of TBZ's own CYP2D6 metabolic clearance (kp_HTBZ, renamed FCONV_HTBZ, despite being labeled a partition coefficient in the original's own comment). Refactored: archetype 1 (no depot, single compartment, linear elimination). EFFECT_HTBZ split out from the original's shared 3-drug Hill curve (EC50_VMAT2/Emax_VMAT2/Hill_VMAT2, applied to a summed HTBZ+DTBZ+VBZ concentration) into its own independently-driveable copy (EC50_HTBZ/EMAX_HTBZ/GAMMA_HTBZ, same values), combined with DTBZ/VBZ only at the point of use via Bliss independence (see refactor notes). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api on the original's own TBZ_25mg scenario, exact match (max abs diff 0.0). Same two build defects as the DTBZ row above, logged translations/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #87/#88 |
| huntingtons-disease | RILUZOLE | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Glutamate release/excitotoxicity (presynaptic Na+-channel/glutamate-release inhibition; modest antichoreic/neuroprotective benefit in this model) | C_RILUZOLE (== PERI_RILUZOLE) / EFFECT_RILUZOLE | compartment amount, not divided by volume -- see notes | Archetype 1 (no depot; KA_RILUZOLE/F_RILUZOLE declared but never wired into a dxdt_ line, preserved unused) plus the same one-way brain-exposure compartment pattern as DTBZ/VBZ. EFFECT_RILUZOLE folds the original's hardcoded inner IMAX ceiling (0.20) and outer scaling factor (0.15) into one EMAX_RILUZOLE=0.03 (rename/fold, not a fit) -- the original's OWN declared Emax_riluzole=0.45 was never actually wired to this formula (dead parameter), logged translations/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #89. Concentration threshold gate (C_RILUZOLE>0.01) preserved exactly. None of the original's own 7 named scenarios ever doses riluzole -- verified instead via a bespoke run (50mg q12h into CENT_RILUZOLE, an arbitrary nonzero dose for an already-declared, previously-unexercised compartment) against qspserver mrgsolve_api, exact match (max abs diff 0.0). Same two build defects as the DTBZ row above, logged translations/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #87/#88 |

| hypercalcemia-of-malignancy | Parathyroid (PTH) | Not a drug -- endogenous disease state | N/A | N/A | N/A | Checked per the task's specific flag: PTH is a $CMT compartment produced entirely by the model's own parathyroid gland dynamics (dxdt_PTH driven by CaSR-set-point secretion S_PTH plus the ectopic-secretion aetiology term PTH_ECT); no ev()/dataset dosing route targets PTH anywhere in the file's 23 scenarios or R driver (confirmed by exact-name grep). Three named effects from one concentration (not TMDD, not a second tissue site), renames of the original's already-Hill-shaped inh_P_TGFb/inh_P_M2/inh_P_ROS (gamma=1, no fit) -- the original's own parameter-sharing quirk preserved exactly (EFFECT_PIR_M2 reuses EMAX_PIR_TGFB, EFFECT_PIR_ROS reuses EC50_PIR_TGFB; no separate EMAX_PIR_M2/EC50_PIR_ROS ever existed). C_PIR/EFFECT_PIR_* kept in $MAIN (not $ODE) to reproduce mrgsolve's one-interval-late $MAIN lag exactly. The census's "duplicate concentration sites" ($MAIN's C_PIR vs $TABLE's independent Cp_pirf recompute, same formula) is real but NOT normalized away -- $TABLE runs unlagged at actual output time while $MAIN is one interval stale, so collapsing them would change Cp_pirf's reported values; kept as two deliberately-separate sites (see refactor notes). N has the identical $MAIN/$TABLE duplication the census did not flag. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api on the original's own 5 scenarios, shortened to a 0-96h window (see N's row for why): exact match, max abs/rel deviation 0.0, 27 shared outputs. "DEG" is a substring fragment shared by twelve unrelated first-order degradation-rate constants scattered across the file (kLTAG_deg, kROS_deg, kNRF2_deg, kER_deg, kTNF_deg, kIL6_deg, kIL1_deg, kTGFb_deg, kCol_deg, kFGF19_deg, kLPS_deg, kMCP1_deg); closest textual match to "ER Stress" is kER_deg (an endogenous ER-stress resolution rate, not a dosed compound). Same defect shape as the "EL" false positive in drug-induced-liver-injury. Left entirely untouched (not part of this refactor's scope, since it is not a compound at all). See nafld_refactor_notes.md |
| nafld-masld | GLP-1 RA (semaglutide-like, GLP1) [classifier had truncated the compound name to "GLP"; the code's own stem is GLP1, see notes] | Normalize duplicate concentration sites, then redirect | GLP-1 receptor agonism (insulin-resistance, hepatic DNL, Kupffer activation) | C_GLP1 / EFFECT_GLP1_IR, EFFECT_GLP1_DNL, EFFECT_GLP1_Kup | ug/mL (plasma) | Refactored 2026-08-31: Archetype 3 minus peripheral (depot+central, linear); GUT_GLP1/CENT_GLP1 already matched the naming convention in the original, only ka_glp1/CL_glp1/V1_glp1/Emax_GLP1_*/E_GLP1_* renamed. Three separate downstream effects (IR, DNL, Kupffer), each its own named EFFECT_GLP1_*, sharing one EC50_GLP1 as the original did; GAMMA_GLP1_*=1 (new, rename not a fit — original was already the plain Hill ratio). The $MAIN-vs-$TABLE duplicate concentration site was NOT collapsed (see notes — collapsing would inject a start-of-interval timing lag into the report, per the ipf_refactor_notes.md precedent); $TABLE's syntax was instead normalized from inline `capture NAME = EXPR;` to `double`+one consolidated $CAPTURE list. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against the file's own "Semaglutide 2.4mg/wk" and "OCA + Semaglutide" scenarios (full 2-year/732-733 pt horizon), exact match, max abs diff 0.0, including at every weekly dose-coincident reporting instant. Two pre-existing upstream mrgsolve-2.0.1 build defects found and fixed syntax-only (UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #119, #120), disclosed in nafld_refactor_notes.md |

Archetype 2. VEGF neutralization is a first-order mass-action sink on the VEGF biomarker compartment (VEGF_bind_BEV, was BEV_effect), but unlike cervical-cancer's BEV row, this file has NO downstream tumor/CA-125/CD8T equation that reads VEGF or any BEV-derived quantity at all -- nothing to rename into an EFFECT_BEV, so none was invented. C_BEV/VEGF_bind_BEV still exposed and captured. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api across all 6 of the original's own scenarios: exact match at floating-point scale (max abs diff 0.0024 on CA125_lvl, order ~300). Pre-existing upstream build defects ($CMT+$INIT duplication, 6 dead $ODE state clamps, 2 $PARAM/`<CMT>_0` name collisions, 1 $ODE-local/$TABLE-capture name collision) fixed syntax-only in the delivered _refactored.R; logged as UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #79, which also documents that bevacizumab has no mechanistic tumor effect in this file despite being a named PAOLA-1 arm |
| ovarian-cancer | Carboplatin | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | DNA crosslinking | C_CAR | µg/mL (plasma) | Archetype 2 (no depot, 2-compartment linear); C_CAR is a direct alias of CENT_CAR, not CENT_CAR/V1_CAR -- the original's own dosing already divides amt by V1 and its own $TABLE captures the compartment directly as "the concentration." Feeds Pt_DNA adduct formation (unchanged, linear turnover, not a Hill ratio) -- direct alias, no fit needed. EFFECT_NIRA renamed from a guarded inline ratio (if(NIRA_C1>0.1) 0.7*NIRA_C1/(NIRA_C1+2000)), already Hill-shaped (EMAX_NIRA=0.7/EC50_NIRA=2000/GAMMA_NIRA=1) -- rename, not a fit; guard threshold preserved exactly. EFFECT_IBU is the original's occD addend (UIBUE/KI_IBU) pulled out and named, NOT independently Hill-bounded — ibuprofen and indomethacin genuinely compete for one COX channel, so the shared saturation (occD=EFFECT_IBU+EFFECT_IND; ICHAN=occD/(1+occD)) is real pharmacology kept at the one combination point, not flattened into two fake independent Hill curves (see refactor notes for the full argument). Two independent disease-facing effects kept as separate named terms (not one compound's mechanisms buried together -- c.f. doxycycline's own 5 FDOX* terms elsewhere in this file): EFFECT_ISO_SEB = rename of the original's already-Hill(gamma=1) ISOEMAX*CISO/(ISOEC50+CISO); EFFECT_ISO_INN (EMAX_ISO_INN=1, new but forced by the original's own algebra, not a free choice) with FISOA = 1-EFFECT_ISO_INN reproducing the original's FISOA = 1/(1+CISO/ISOAIC50) exactly. C_ISO/EFFECT_ISO_SEB/EFFECT_ISO_INN declared in $GLOBAL, not $PARAM (same read-only-reference constraint as copd/bc), exposed via $CAPTURE. No pre-existing build defect -- original compiled cleanly, nothing logged to UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api reproducing the original's own S12 scenario (isotretinoin 20mg/day x16wk, PPR-severe phenotype): 33/36 shared outputs exact match (max abs diff 0.0) incl. the renamed PK compartments themselves; 3 outputs (CEA, DLQI, ERYIDX) showed max abs diff 1.0e-4 at isolated timepoints, at the API's own JSON rounding floor (pow(x,1.0) libm noise), not a structural mismatch |

| stevens-johnson-syndrome-ten | Culprit drug (DRUG) [generic/illustrative "carbamazepine-like" causative agent, not a single named real drug -- genuinely dosed via ev() into its own depot, not a pure trigger/initial-condition mechanism, see notes] | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | HLA class I (hapten/antigen-presentation trigger) | GUT_DRUG / CENT_DRUG | C_DRUG (mg/L) / EFFECT_DRUG (linear, not Hill -- see notes) | Refactored 2026-08-30: Archetype 3 minus peripheral (depot+central, linear), rename-only, values unchanged. The census's "Culprit drug" name is accurate (unlike the SNMP/PB-style mislabelings elsewhere in this table) -- this compartment is a genuine, independently-dosed PK pair (`ev(cmt="A_drug_dep")` in the file's own R driver), not the disease's endogenous antigen state. EFFECT_DRUG = hla_amp*C_DRUG is bespoke: the original's own driving term (`KON_HLA*hla_amp*Cdrug` into the Ag_HLA ODE) is linear mass-action, not a saturating Hill/Emax shape, so no EMAX/EC50/GAMMA_DRUG were invented for it. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api on all 7 of the file's own named scenarios (Supportive/IVIG/Cyclosporine/Etanercept/Methylpred/CSA_ETAN/JAK_inhib, each with the culprit drug's own 400mg q12h x3 dosing, 30d window): exact match, max abs diff 0.0, on 6 of 7; the IVIG scenario differs by <=1e-4 abs at 5/302 timepoints (rounding-boundary floating-point noise from the pow(x,1.0) Hill rewrite elsewhere in the same ODE system, self-corrects by end of run) -- see sjsten_refactor_notes.md. Also flags a pre-existing, non-blocking dead-code defect in this same block (drug_input/PREDOSE/SCEN_WD/DAY_WD computed but never used) -- UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #98. |

EMAX_CSA/EC50_CSA/GAMMA_CSA new named parameters pulled out of the original's already-Hill-shaped inline ratio (rename, not a refit; GAMMA=1, no explicit exponent in the original). | type1-diabetes | INS | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | ? | CENT_INS | C_INS (mU/L) | Refactored 2026-08-30: bespoke structure (double-depot SC absorption [GUT1_INS hexamer -> GUT2_INS dimer/monomer] into a central pool CENT_INS already stored in concentration units, plus a genuinely separate peripheral distribution compartment PERI_INS via micro rate constants K12_INS/K21_INS -- kept as micro-constants rather than forced into CL/Q/V since the declared "peripheral volume" V2_INS is dead/unused in the original and does not reciprocally balance K12_INS*V1_INS against K21_INS*V2_INS). CENT_INS also receives fused endogenous beta-cell secretion (secR) and the closed-loop-pump infusion term, both preserved unchanged (parallels the diabetic-ketoacidosis Riv fused-flux precedent). Insulin drives three independently-named, purely linear (non-saturating) disease-facing effect terms -- EFFECT_INS_EGP (hepatic glucose output suppression), EFFECT_INS_UP (glucose uptake stimulation, via the lagged effect compartment EFF_INS), EFFECT_INS_GCG (glucagon suppression, read directly off C_INS, not the lagged compartment) -- kept linear rather than forced into a Hill/Emax shape since the original has no saturation term at all (a Hill formula cannot represent an unbounded linear term without misrepresenting it). Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against 4 scenarios built from the original's own bolus_event/meal_event dosing constructors and parameter overrides (undosed baseline; 2-day MDI-style SC bolus+basal; combined insulin+teplizumab): exact match, max absolute/relative deviation 0.0 on every shared compartment output. Found and logged, not fixed upstream (translations/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md #42): the original does not compile as-is under mrgsolve 2.0.1 ($CMT and $INIT jointly redeclare all 20 compartments). Also noted (not upstream-logged, non-blocking): two declared-but-unused parameters (V2_INS, ka_type) and a harmless duplicate dxdt_CENT_INS assignment (dead first attempt, silently overwritten by the second) -- see t1dm_refactor_notes.md. |
| type1-diabetes | TEP | Redirect concentration inside #define macro | ? | CENT_TEP | C_TEP (ug/mL) | Refactored 2026-08-30: Archetype 1 (no depot, single compartment, linear elimination), central state already stored directly in concentration units (C_TEP = CENT_TEP, no /V1_TEP division needed, same "already-a-concentration" pattern as diabetic-ketoacidosis's CENT_INS). The census's "inside #define macro" classification does not describe the actual redirect site: `#define TEPLIZ (CENT_TEP > 0.001)` is dead code, never referenced anywhere else in the file -- the real (and only) concentration reads are two plain $ODE lines multiplying CENT_TEP directly. Two disease-facing effect terms, EFFECT_TEP_CTL (CTL-suppression rate) and EFFECT_TEP_TREG (Treg-expansion rate), both purely linear in C_TEP with no saturation term in the original -- kept linear (rename only), not forced into a Hill/Emax shape, for the same reason as INS's effect terms. Verified via qspserver mrgsolve_api against the original's own tepliz_dose() 14-dose IV course (doses spaced 1440 min apart, exactly reproducing its own day*1440 time convention) plus a combined insulin+teplizumab run: exact match, max absolute/relative deviation 0.0 on every shared compartment output. Shares upstream defect #42 with INS (same file, same $CMT+$INIT duplication, logged once). Also noted (not upstream-logged): the original's own tepliz_dose()/bolus dosing adds the raw dose amount directly into the concentration-labeled CENT_TEP state without dividing by V1_TEP (V1_TEP is used only in the elimination-rate term) -- a pre-existing dimensional inconsistency, preserved exactly, not introduced or corrected by this refactor -- see t1dm_refactor_notes.md. |

| visceral-leishmaniasis | Amphotericin B disposition (FRE) | Redirect concentration (clean single site) | Ergosterol (parasite/fungal membrane sterol); the free-plasma pool itself also drives nephrotoxicity | CENT_FRE / PERI_FRE / TISSUE_SP_AMB, TISSUE_LI_AMB, TISSUE_BM_AMB, TISSUE_SK_AMB, TISSUE_KID_AMB | C_FRE (mg/L, free/deoxycholate plasma, was C_SB5-style local "C_FRE" already in the original) / EFFECT_AMB_SP, EFFECT_AMB_LI, EFFECT_AMB_BM, EFFECT_AMB_SK (0-1, +*Q quiescent-population variants kept internal) | Refactored: real, not mislabeled -- FRE and LIP (see that row) are two genuinely distinct administered products (free/deoxycholate vs liposomal) that converge on the SAME shared TISSUE_*_AMB intramacrophage/renal pools, because the parasite and renal tubule cannot tell which formulation delivered the molecule. Archetype-3-like two-compartment (CENT_FRE+PERI_FRE, was A_FRE/A_PER) plus the 5 shared tissue sites (TISSUE_SP/LI/BM/SK/KID_AMB, was AMB_SP/LI/BM/SK/KID) fed by C_FRE's passive Kp equilibration (renamed KPSP_FRE etc.) and by LIP's phagocytic "mps" deposit. EFFECT_AMB_<organ> (EMAX_AMB/EC50_AMB/GAMMA_AMB, rename not refit) reads the converged pool regardless of formulation. Renal toxicity (TUBI/SCR/KSER/MGSER) reads the shared TISSUE_KID_AMB pool. See vl_refactor_notes.md. |
